\+\_\+sock\+\_\+tx\+\_\+timestamp -\/ checks whether the outgoing packet is to be time stamped @sk\+: socket sending this packet @tsflags\+: timestamping flags to use @tx\+\_\+flags\+: completed with instructions for time stamping @tskey\+: filled in with next sk\+\_\+tskey (not for T\+CP, which uses seqno)

Note\+: callers should take care of initial {\ttfamily $\ast$tx\+\_\+flags} value (usually 0) Here is the call graph for this function\+:

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}} lock\+\_\+sock\+\_\+fast (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsock}{sock}} $\ast$}]{sk }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

lock\+\_\+sock\+\_\+fast -\/ fast version of lock\+\_\+sock @sk\+: socket

This version should be used for very small section, where process wont block return false if fast path is taken\+:

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{sk\_page\_frag()}{sk\_page\_frag()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structpage__frag}{page\+\_\+frag}}$\ast$ sk\+\_\+page\+\_\+frag (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsock}{sock}} $\ast$}]{sk }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

sk\+\_\+page\+\_\+frag -\/ return an appropriate \mbox{\hyperlink{structpage__frag}{page\+\_\+frag}} @sk\+: socket

Use the per task \mbox{\hyperlink{structpage__frag}{page\+\_\+frag}} instead of the per socket one for optimization when we know that we\textquotesingle{}re in the normal context and owns everything that\textquotesingle{}s associated with current.

gfpflags\+\_\+allow\+\_\+blocking() isn\textquotesingle{}t enough here as direct reclaim may nest inside other socket operations and end up recursing into \mbox{\hyperlink{net_2sock_8h_a21188fdb9486b1b53d899a4742ccb12e}{sk\+\_\+page\+\_\+frag()}} while it\textquotesingle{}s already in use.

Return\+: a per task \mbox{\hyperlink{structpage__frag}{page\+\_\+frag}} if context allows that, otherwise a per socket one. \mbox{\Hypertarget{net_2sock_8h_a3e68cf2f7cf626f9c449e1b3ff723ddc}\label{net_2sock_8h_a3e68cf2f7cf626f9c449e1b3ff723ddc}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{skwq\_has\_sleeper()}{skwq\_has\_sleeper()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}} skwq\+\_\+has\+\_\+sleeper (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsocket__wq}{socket\+\_\+wq}} $\ast$}]{wq }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

skwq\+\_\+has\+\_\+sleeper -\/ check if there are any waiting processes @wq\+: struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsocket__wq}{socket\+\_\+wq}}

Return\+: true if \mbox{\hyperlink{structsocket__wq}{socket\+\_\+wq}} has waiting processes

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} purpose of the skwq\+\_\+has\+\_\+sleeper and sock\+\_\+poll\+\_\+wait is to wrap the memory barrier call. They were added due to the race found within the tcp code.

Consider following tcp code paths\+:\+:

C\+P\+U1 C\+P\+U2 sys\+\_\+select receive packet ... ... \+\_\+\+\_\+add\+\_\+wait\+\_\+queue update tp-\/$>$rcv\+\_\+nxt ... ... tp-\/$>$rcv\+\_\+nxt check sock\+\_\+def\+\_\+readable ... \{ schedule \mbox{\hyperlink{rcupdate_8h_a039341af5d714114b6c2eb8e6ed305e6}{rcu\+\_\+read\+\_\+lock()}}; wq = rcu\+\_\+dereference(sk-\/$>$sk\+\_\+wq); if (wq \&\& waitqueue\+\_\+active(\&wq-\/$>$wait)) wake\+\_\+up\+\_\+interruptible(\&wq-\/$>$wait) ... \}

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} race for tcp fires when the \+\_\+\+\_\+add\+\_\+wait\+\_\+queue changes done by C\+P\+U1 stay in its cache, and so does the tp-\/$>$rcv\+\_\+nxt update on C\+P\+U2 side. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} C\+P\+U1 could then endup calling schedule and sleep forever if there are no more data on the socket. \mbox{\Hypertarget{net_2sock_8h_a748879a98032083d651315fc7a3b0768}\label{net_2sock_8h_a748879a98032083d651315fc7a3b0768}}
